# Recording script: Ashkenazi (liturgical)

Generated by tools/audio/recording_script.mjs from data/audio/manifest.json. Do not edit by hand; re-run the tool.

No recordings exist yet. Every file below is a request.

## How to use

- Record each voice talent (m = man, f = woman) separately. File name = path below under `audio/<voice>/`; i.e. the full path is `audio/m/ashkenazi/<kind>/<id>.mp3` and `audio/f/...`.
- Deliver 48 kHz / 24-bit WAV masters named like the path (`<id>.wav`), one file per clip, plus the CSV filled in. Lane tooling will normalize and encode (tools/audio/normalize.sh).
- Record the take count shown for every clip and mark the preferred take; keep head/tail silence of about 0.3-0.5 s.
- Pointed Hebrew is the authority for what to say. IPA is shown only where the project data supplies it (lane-4 starter values for Israeli syllables and consonants; NOT native-checked).

## Ashkenazi header (read before recording)

- Switches from the project brief: tav without dagesh = s; kamatz = o; penultimate (milel) stress in prayer reading.
- OPEN DECISION: dialect for cholam / tzere (e.g. oy vs ey) and for the vowel set generally (Litvish vs Polish/Hasidic). Choose one dialect with the talent and keep it for every clip. This is not settled by any source in the project notes.
- No TTS can produce this tradition, so every Ashkenazi clip is a human-recording task.

## Letter variant forms (vet, khaf, fe, sin)

Takes per clip: 3. Clips: 4.

| id | file (under audio/<voice>/) | pointed Hebrew | IPA | direction |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| vet | ashkenazi/letter/vet.mp3 | בֵית |  | VARIANT FORM NAME: say the name with the variant sound, once, falling intonation (vet = with a v, khaf = with the ch of chet, fe = with an f, sin = with an s). Record right after the plain form (bet/vet, kaf/khaf, pe/fe, shin/sin) so the contrast is clear. Ashkenazi: use your community's names; DIRECTOR to confirm. |
| khaf | ashkenazi/letter/khaf.mp3 | כַף |  | (as above) |
| fe | ashkenazi/letter/fe.mp3 | פֵא |  | (as above) |
| sin | ashkenazi/letter/sin.mp3 | שִׂין |  | (as above) |

|---|---|---|---|---|
| kamatz | ashkenazi/vowel/kamatz.mp3 | אָ | [ɔ] | ISOLATED VOWEL: the vowel sound alone on a soft onset (the carrier letter is silent), 0.6-1.0 s, level pitch. shva: record the pronounced shva as a very short e-like vowel; the director may ask for an extra "silent shva" demonstration. dagesh / shin-dot / sin-dot: say the sound the mark produces on its carrier (bet with dagesh "b", shin "sh", sin "s"). Ashkenazi: kamatz is "o"; cholam and tzere per the chosen dialect (see script header). |

| vahavta-full | ashkenazi/prayer/vahavta-full.mp3 | וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל־לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל־נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל־מְאֹדֶךָ׃ וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם עַל־לְבָבֶךָ׃ וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ׃ וּקְשַׁרְתָּם לְאוֹת עַל־יָדֶךָ וְהָיוּ לְטֹטָפֹת בֵּין עֵינֶיךָ׃ וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל־מְזוּזֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ׃ |  | (as above) |

| adon-olam-1 | ashkenazi/prayer/adon-olam-1.mp3 | אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר מָלַךְ בְּטֶרֶם כָּל יְצִיר נִבְרָא׃ לְעֵת נַעֲשָׂה בְחֶפְצוֹ כֹּל אֲזַי מֶלֶךְ שְׁמוֹ נִקְרָא׃ וְאַחֲרֵי כִּכְלוֹת הַכֹּל לְבַדּוֹ יִמְלֹךְ נוֹרָא׃ וְהוּא הָיָה וְהוּא הֹוֶה וְהוּא יִהְיֶה בְּתִפְאָרָה׃ וְהוּא אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי לְהַמְשִׁיל לוֹ לְהַחְבִּירָה׃ בְּלִי רֵאשִׁית בְּלִי תַכְלִית וְלוֹ הָעֹז וְהַמִּשְׂרָה׃ וְהוּא אֵלִי וְחַי גוֹאֲלִי וְצוּר חֶבְלִי בְּעֵת צָרָה׃ וְהוּא נִסִּי וּמָנוֹס לִי מְנָת כּוֹסִי בְּיוֹם אֶקְרָא׃ בְּיָדוֹ אַפְקִיד רוּחִי בְּעֵת אִישָׁן וְאָעִירָה׃ וְעִם רוּחִי גְּוִיָּתִי אֲדֹנָי לִי וְלֹא אִירָא׃ |  | (as above) |
| kaddish-1 | ashkenazi/prayer/kaddish-1.mp3 | יִתְגַּדַּל וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא אָמֵן בְּעָלְמָא דִּי־בְרָא כִרְעוּתֵהּ וְיַמְלִיךְ מַלְכוּתֵהּ בְּחַיֵּיכוֹן וּבְיוֹמֵיכוֹן וּבְחַיֵּי דְכָל־בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּעֲגָלָא וּבִזְמַן קָרִיב וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ יְהֵא שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא מְבָרַךְ לְעָלַם וּלְעָלְמֵי עָלְמַיָּא יִתְבָּרַךְ וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח וְיִתְפָּאַר וְיִתְרוֹמַם וְיִתְנַשֵּׂא וְיִתְהַדָּר וְיִתְעַלֶּה וְיִתְהַלָּל שְׁמֵהּ דְקוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא לְעֵלָּא מִן־כָּל־בִּרְכָתָא וְשִׁירָתָא תֻּשְׁבְּחָתָא וְנֶחָמָתָא דַּאֲמִירָן בְּעָלְמָא וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ יְהֵא שְׁלָמָא רַבָּא מִן־שְׁמַיָּא וְחַיִּים עָלֵינוּ וְעַל־כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ עוֹשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו הוּא יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם עָלֵינוּ וְעַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ |  | (as above) |

## Minimal pairs and confusable letters

These reuse the syllable-grid files above; no extra clips. Record the two members back to back so the contrast is natural.

- bet / vav: Distinct sounds: record pairs back to back (A, B) in the existing syllable files; do not exaggerate. Syllables: bet-a/vav-a, bet-e/vav-e, bet-i/vav-i, bet-o/vav-o, bet-u/vav-u.
- kaf / qof: Distinct sounds: record pairs back to back (A, B) in the existing syllable files; do not exaggerate. Syllables: kaf-a/qof-a, kaf-e/qof-e, kaf-i/qof-i, kaf-o/qof-o, kaf-u/qof-u.
- chet / kaf: Distinct sounds: record pairs back to back (A, B) in the existing syllable files; do not exaggerate. Syllables: chet-a/kaf-a, chet-e/kaf-e, chet-i/kaf-i, chet-o/kaf-o, chet-u/kaf-u.
- tet / tav: Distinct sounds: record pairs back to back (A, B) in the existing syllable files; do not exaggerate. Syllables: tet-a/tav-a, tet-e/tav-e, tet-i/tav-i, tet-o/tav-o, tet-u/tav-u.
- alef / ayin: Distinct sounds: record pairs back to back (A, B) in the existing syllable files; do not exaggerate. Syllables: alef-a/ayin-a, alef-e/ayin-e, alef-i/ayin-i, alef-o/ayin-o, alef-u/ayin-u.
